They reached Barney by telephone, but he was unable to give any information that was of the slightest use. All that he knew was that the printing works were supposed to be somewhere in the West.

“Johnny knows more about it than I do, or than anybody. All the boys agree as to that,” said Barney. “They told him a lot in ‘boob.’ ”

Leaving Peter to return home, Mr. Reeder made a call at Johnny’s flat. Parker was up. He had been notified earlier in the morning of his master’s disappearance, but he had no explanation to offer.

===Chinese===
他们通过电话联系上了巴尼，但他无法提供任何有用的信息。他所知道的只是印刷厂据说在西部的某个地方。

"约翰尼比我知道得多，比任何人都多。所有兄弟都同意这一点，"巴尼说，"他们在'监狱'（boob）里告诉了他很多。"

让彼得回家后，里德先生去了约翰尼的公寓。帕克（Parker）已经起床了。他今天早上早些时候就得知主人失踪了，但无法提供任何解释。

===Original===
He was preparing to give a list of the clothes that Johnny had been wearing, but Reeder cut him short impatiently.

“Try to think of Mr. Gray as a human being, and not as a tailor’s dummy,” he said wrathfully. “You realise that he is in very grave danger?”

“I am not at all worried, sir,” said the precise Parker. “Mr. Gray was wearing his new sock suspenders—”

For once Mr. Reeder forgot himself.

“You’re a damned fool, Parker,” he said.

“I hope not, sir,” said Parker as he bowed him out.

===Chinese===
他正准备列出约翰尼当时穿的衣服清单，但里德不耐烦地打断了他。

"试着把格雷先生当一个人来想，而不是裁缝店里的人体模型，"他愤怒地说，"你意识到他正处在非常危险的境地吗？"

"我一点也不担心，先生，"一丝不苟的帕克说，"格雷先生穿着他的新袜子吊带——"

这一次里德先生失态了。

"你真是个该死的傻瓜，帕克，"他说。

"但愿不是，先生，"帕克一边鞠躬送他出去一边说。

## XXIX

===Original===
It was five minutes past two in the morning when Marney, sitting in the drawing-room at the front of the house, heard the sound of a motorcar stop before the house. Going into the hall, she opened the door, and, standing on the step, peered into the darkness.
"Is that you, father?" she asked.
There was no reply, and she walked quickly up the garden path to the gate. The car was a closed coupe, and as she looked over the gate, she saw a hand come out and beckon her, and heard a voice whisper:
"Don't make a noise. Come in here; I want to talk to you. I don't want Barney to see me."

===Chinese===
凌晨两点零五分，玛妮（Marney）坐在房子前面的客厅里，听见一辆汽车在门前停了下来。她走进门厅，打开大门，站在台阶上朝黑暗中张望。
"是你吗，爸爸？"她问。
没有人回答。她快步沿着花园小径走到门口。那是一辆封闭式的双座马车，她朝门口望去时，看见一只手伸出来朝她招了招，听到一个声音低声说：
"别出声。过来；我要跟你谈谈。我不想让巴尼（Barney）看到我。"

===Original===
Bewildered, she obeyed. Jerking open the door, she jumped into the dark interior, by the side of the man at the wheel.
"What is it?" she asked.
Then, to her amazement, the car began to move toward the main road. It had evidently circled before it had stopped.
"What is the matter, father?" she asked.
And then she heard a low chuckle that made her blood run cold.
"Go into the back and stay there. If you make a row, I'll spoil that complexion of yours, Marney Legge!"
"Jeffrey!" she gasped.

===Chinese===
她满心困惑，还是照做了。猛地拉开车门，她跳进了漆黑的车内，坐到方向盘旁的男人身边。
"什么事？"她问道。
接着，令她大吃一惊的是，汽车开始朝大路驶去。显然车在停下来之前已经绕过一圈了。
"怎么了，爸爸？"她问。
然后她听到一阵低沉的笑声，让她浑身血液都冰冷了。
"坐到后排去，老实待着。你要是敢闹，我就把你那张漂亮脸蛋给毁了，玛妮·莱格（Marney Legge）！"
"杰弗里！"她倒抽一口气。

===Original===
She gripped the inside handle of the door and had half turned it when he caught her with his disengaged hand and flung her into the back of the car.
"I'll kill you if you make me do that again." There was a queer little sob of pain in his voice, and she remembered his wound.
"Where are you taking me?" she asked.
"I'm taking you to your father," was the unexpected reply. "Will you sit quiet? If you try to get away, or attempt to call assistance, I'll drive you at full speed into the first tree I see and we'll finish the thing together."

===Chinese===
她抓住车门内侧的把手，正要拧开，他腾出一只手抓住她，把她甩到后座去。
"你要是再逼我动手，我就杀了你。"他声音里带着一种奇怪的痛苦呜咽，她想起了他的伤口。
"你要带我去哪儿？"她问。
"带你去你父亲那里，"这个出人意料的回答说，"你能不能老实坐着？你要是想逃跑，或者想叫人帮忙，我就全速朝我看到的第一棵树撞过去，咱们一起了结。"

===Original===
From the ferocity of his tone she did not doubt that he would carry his threat into execution. Mile after mile the car sped on, flashing through villages, slowing through the sparsely peopled streets of small towns. It was nearing three o'clock when they came into the street of a town and, looking through the window, she saw a grey façade and knew she was in Oxford.
In ten minutes they were through the city and traversing the main western road. And now, for the first time, Jeffrey Legge became communicative.
"You've never been in 'boob,' have you, angel?" he asked.

===Chinese===
从他语气里的凶狠，她毫不怀疑他会说到做到。汽车一英里一英里地疾驰，穿过一座座村庄，在人烟稀少的小镇街道上放慢速度。将近三点钟的时候，他们驶进一座小镇的街道，她透过车窗望去，看到一排灰色的建筑立面，知道自己到了牛津（Oxford）。
十分钟后，他们穿过了这座城市，行驶在向西的主干道上。这时，杰弗里·莱格（Jeffrey Legge）第一次打开了话匣子。
"你从来没蹲过'监狱'（boob），对吧，小天使？"他问。

===Original===
She did not answer.
"Never been inside the little birdhouse with the other canaries, eh? Well, that's an experience ahead of you. I am going to put you in jail, kid. Peter's never been in jail either, but he nearly had the experience tonight."
"I don't believe you," she said. "My father has not broken the law."
"Not for a long time, at any rate," agreed Jeffrey, dexterously lighting a cigarette with one hand. "But there's a little 'boob' waiting for him all right now."
"A prison," she said, incredulously. "I don't believe you."

===Chinese===
她没有回答。
"从来没跟那些金丝雀一起进过鸟笼子，嗯？好吧，这是一段等着你的经历。我要把你关进监狱，小丫头。彼得也从来没蹲过监狱，不过他今晚差点就体验到了。"
"我不信你，"她说，"我父亲没有犯法。"
"至少很长一段时间没犯，"杰弗里同意道，一只手灵巧地点燃了一支烟，"不过现在确实有一间小'监狱'在等着他呢。"
"监狱，"她难以置信地说，"我不信你。"

===Original===
"You've said that twice, and you're the only person living that's called me a liar that number of times."
He turned off into a side road, and for a quarter of an hour gave her opportunity for thought.
"It might interest you to know that Johnny is there," he said. "Dear little Johnny! The easiest crook that ever fell—and this time he's got a lifer."

===Chinese===
"你说了两遍了，你是活着的人里面唯一一个敢骂我骗子骂了这么多遍的。"
他拐进一条岔路，给了她一刻钟的时间思考。
"你可能有兴趣知道约翰尼也在里面，"他说，"亲爱的小约翰尼！有史以来最容易上钩的骗子——这次他判了终身监禁。"

===Original===
The car began to move down a sharp declivity, and, looking through the rain-spattered windscreen, she saw a squat, dark building ahead.
"Here we are," he said, as the car stopped.
Looking through the window she saw, with a gasp of astonishment, that he had spoken the truth. They were at the door of a prison. The great, black, iron-studded gates were opening as she looked, and the car passed through under the deep archway and stopped.
"Get down," said Jeff, and she obeyed.

===Chinese===
汽车开始沿着陡坡往下驶去，透过溅满雨水的挡风玻璃，她看到前方有一座低矮的黑色建筑。
"到了，"车停下时他说。
她朝车窗望去，惊讶地倒吸一口气——他说的是实话。他们站在一座监狱的大门前。她看着那扇巨大的、黑色的、嵌满铁钉的铁门正在缓缓打开，汽车从深深的拱门下穿过去，停了下来。
"下去，"杰夫说。她照做了。

===Original===
A narrow black door led from the archway, and, following her, he caught her by the arm and pushed her through. She was in a narrow room, the walls of which were covered with stained and discoloured whitewash. A large fireplace, overflowing with ashes, a rickety chair and a faded board screwed to the wall were the only furniture. In the dim light of a carbon lamp she saw the almost indistinguishable words: "His Majesty's Prison, Keytown," and beneath, row after row of closely set regulations. A rough-looking, powerfully-built man had followed her into the room, which was obviously the gatekeeper's lodge.
"Have you got the cell ready?"
"Yes, I have," said the man. "Does she want anything to eat?"
"If she does, she'll want," said Jeff curtly.

===Chinese===
拱门里有一扇窄窄的黑门。他跟在她身后，抓住她的胳膊把她推了进去。她置身于一间狭小的房间，墙壁上满是斑驳褪色的白灰。一个溢满灰烬的大壁炉、一把摇摇欲坠的椅子和一块用螺丝拧在墙上的褪色板子，是这里仅有的家具。在一盏炭灯的昏暗光线下，她看到了几乎难以辨认的字样："国王陛下基镇（Keytown）监狱"，下面是一排排密密麻麻的规章制度。一个相貌粗犷、体格强壮的男人跟在她身后进了房间——这显然是看门人的门房。
"牢房准备好了吗？"
"准备好了，"那人说，"她要吃东西吗？"
"她要是要，就给她，"杰夫冷冷地说。

===Original===
He took off his greatcoat and hung it on a nail, and then, with Jeffrey's hand gripping her arm, she was led again into the archway and across a small courtyard, through an iron grille gate and a further door.
A solitary light that burnt in a bracket near the door, showed her that she was in a small hall. Around this, at the height of about nine feet from the ground, ran a gallery, which was reached by a flight of iron stairs. There was no need to ask what was the meaning of those two rows of black doors that punctured the wall. They were cells. She was in a prison!

===Chinese===
他脱下大衣挂在钉子上，然后杰弗里再次攥住她的胳膊，带她穿过拱门，走过一个小庭院，经过一道铁栅栏门和另一扇门。
门旁一盏孤灯照亮了她所在的小厅。大约九英尺高的地方，环绕小厅有一圈走廊，通向那里的是铁楼梯。不需要问墙上那两排黑色小门是什么意思。它们是牢房。她身处一座监狱！

===Original===
While she was wondering what it all meant, a door near at hand was unlocked, and she was pushed in. The cell was a small one, the floor of worn stone, but a new bedstead had been fitted up in one corner. There was a washstand; and, as she was to discover, the cell communicated with another containing a stone bath and washplace.
"The condemned cell," explained Jeffrey Legge with relish. "You'll have plenty of ghosts to keep you company tonight, Marney."
In her heart she was panic-stricken, but she showed none of her fear as she faced him.
"A ghost would be much less repulsive to me than you, Jeffrey Legge," she said, and he seemed taken aback by the spirit she displayed.
"You will have both," he said, as he slammed the door on her and locked it.

===Chinese===
她正纳闷这一切意味着什么，旁边一扇门被打开，她被推了进去。牢房不大，地面是磨旧的石头，但角落里新装了一张床架。有一个洗脸台；而且她后来发现，这间牢房与另一间相通，里面放着一个石制浴缸和洗漱台。
"死囚牢房，"杰弗里·莱格津津有味地解释道，"今晚你可有大把鬼魂陪你了，玛妮。"
她内心惊恐万状，但面对他时没有流露出一丝恐惧。
"鬼魂可比你让我觉得舒服多了，杰弗里·莱格，"她说。他似乎被她的气势镇住了。
"两个你都会有的，"他说着，砰地一声关上门，上了锁。

===Original===
The cell was illuminated by a feeble light that came through an opaque pane of glass by the side of the door. Presently, when her eyes grew accustomed to the semidarkness, she was able to take stock of her surroundings. The prison must have been a very old one, for the walls were at one place worn smooth, probably by the back of some condemned unfortunate who had waited day after day for the hour of doom. She shuddered, as her imagination called to her the agony of soul which these four walls had held.
By standing on the bed she could reach a window. That also was of toughened glass, set in small, rusty frames. Some of the panes were missing, but she guessed that the outlook from the window would not be particularly promising, even supposing she could force the window.

===Chinese===
牢房里的光来自门旁一块不透明玻璃窗透进来的微弱光线。过了一会儿，当她的眼睛适应了半明半暗之后，她开始打量四周。这座监狱一定年代久远，因为有一处墙壁被磨得光滑，大概是什么不幸的死囚日复一日倚靠在那里等候行刑时刻留下的。她打了个寒颤，想象着这四面墙壁曾经吞没了多少灵魂的痛苦。
站在床上她能够到一扇窗户。那也是钢化玻璃，装在生锈的小窗框里。有几块玻璃不见了，但她估计窗外的景色不会好到哪里去——即便她能撬开窗户。

===Original===
The night had been unusually cold and raw for the time of year, and, pulling a blanket from the bed, she wrapped it about her and sat down on the stool, waiting for the light to grow.
And so, sitting, her weary eyes closing involuntarily, she heard a stealthy tapping. It came from above, and her heart fluttered at the thought that possibly, in the cell above her, her father was held… or Johnny.
Climbing on to the bed, she rapped with her knuckles on the stone ceiling. Somebody answered. They were tapping a message in Morse, which she could not understand. Presently the tapping ceased. She heard footsteps above. And then, looking by chance at the broken pane of the window, she saw something come slowly downward and out of view. She leapt up, gripping the window pane, and saw a piece of black silk.

===Chinese===
那晚的气温对于这个季节来说异常寒冷阴沉。她从床上拽过一条毯子裹在身上，在凳子上坐下，等待天亮。
就这样坐着，疲惫的眼睛不由自主地合上时，她听到了一阵轻轻的敲击声。声音从上方传来，她的心怦怦直跳——想到上面那间牢房里可能关着她的父亲……或者约翰尼。
她爬上床，用指关节敲了敲石质天花板。有人回应。他们在用摩尔斯电码敲击信息，她看不懂。不久敲击声停了。她听到头顶传来脚步声。然后，她碰巧望向那块破窗玻璃，看到有什么东西缓缓垂下来，消失在视线之外。她猛地跳起来，抓住窗框，看到一块黑丝绸。

===Original===
With difficulty two fingers touched it at last and drew it gently in through the window pane. She pulled it up, and, as she suspected, found a piece of paper tied to the end. It was a banknote. Bewildered, she gazed at it until it occurred to her that there might be a message written on the other side. The pencil marks were faint, and she carried the note as near to the light as she could get.

> "Who is there? Is it you, Peter? I am up above. Johnny."

She suppressed the cry that rose to her lips. Both Johnny and her father were there. Then Jeffrey had not lied.
How could she answer? She had no pencil. Then she saw that the end of the cotton was weighted by a small piece of pencil, the kind that is found attached to a dance programme. With this unsatisfactory medium she wrote a reply and pushed it through the window, and after a while she saw it drawn up. Johnny was there—and Johnny knew. She felt strangely comforted by his presence, impotent though he was.

===Chinese===
她好不容易用两根手指碰到了它，轻轻把它从窗玻璃旁拉了进来。她抽出来一看，果然如她所料，末端绑着一张纸。是一张钞票。她茫然地盯着看，直到想到背面可能写着字。铅笔字迹很淡，她尽可能把纸条凑到光亮处。

> "谁在那里？是你吗，彼得？我在上面。约翰尼。"

她强忍住涌到嘴边的惊呼。约翰尼和她父亲都在这里。那么杰弗里没有撒谎。
她怎么回答呢？她没有铅笔。然后她看到棉线的末端系着一小截铅笔头——就是舞会节目单上夹着的那种。她用这支不太好使的笔写了一封回信，推过窗户。过了一会儿，她看到回信被拉了上去。约翰尼在那里——而且约翰尼知道了。尽管他无能为力，但他的存在让她莫名地感到安慰。

===Original===
For half an hour she waited at the window, but now the daylight had come, and evidently Johnny thought it was too dangerous to make any further communications.
Exhausted, she lay down on the bed, intending to remain awake, but within five minutes she was sleeping heavily. The sound of a key in the lock made her spring to her feet. It was the man she had seen in the early morning; he was carrying a big tray, set with a clumsy cup and saucer, six slices of bread and butter, and an enormous teapot. He put it down on the bed, for want of a table, and without a word went out. She looked at the little platinum watch on her wrist: it was ten o'clock. Half an hour later the man came and took away the tray.
"Where am I?" she asked.
"You're in 'boob,'" he said with quiet amusement. "But it is better than any other 'boob' you've ever been in, young lady. And don't try to ask me questions, because you'll not get a civil answer if you do."

===Chinese===
她在窗边等了半小时，但天已经亮了，显然约翰尼认为再传递信息太危险了。
筋疲力尽的她躺到床上，本想保持清醒，但不到五分钟就沉沉睡去。钥匙在锁孔里转动的声响让她一跃而起。是她清晨见过的那个人；他端着一个大盘子，上面放着一套笨拙的杯碟、六片黄油面包和一把大茶壶。没有桌子，他把盘子放在床上，一言不发地走了出去。她看了看手腕上那块小铂金表：十点。半小时后那人来收走了盘子。
"我这是在哪儿？"她问。
"你在'监狱'里，"他平静地带着一丝嘲弄说，"不过这可比你待过的任何其他'监狱'都强，小姑娘。别想套我的话，问了也别想听到一句好话。"

===Original===
At two o'clock came another meal, a little more tastily served this time. It seemed, from the appearance of the plate, that Jeffrey had sent into Oxford for a new service specially for her benefit. Again she attempted to discover what had happened to her father, but with no more satisfactory result.
The weary day dragged through; every minute seemed an hour, every hour interminable. Darkness had fallen again when the last of the visits was made, and this time it was Jeffrey Legge. At the sight of his face, all her terror turned to wonder. He was ghastly pale, his eyes burnt strangely, and the hand that came up to his lips was trembling as though he were suffering from a fever. "What do you want?" she asked.
"I want you," he said brokenly. "I want you for the life of my father!"
"What do you mean?" she gasped.
"Peter Kane killed my father last night," he said.
"You're mad," she gasped. "My father is here—you told me."
"I told you a lie. What does it matter what I told you, anyway? Peter Kane escaped on the way to Keytown, and he went back to the club and killed my father!"

===Chinese===
两点钟又送来一顿饭，这次摆盘稍微讲究了一些。从盘子的样式来看，杰弗里显然是特意派人到牛津去买了这套新餐具来伺候她。她又试图打听父亲的下落，但依然没有得到令人满意的答复。
这漫长的一天熬过了；每一分钟都像一个小时，每一个小时都望不到头。最后一次探视时天已经又黑了，来的是杰弗里·莱格。一看到他的脸，她所有的恐惧都变成了惊愕。他面如死灰，眼睛燃烧着异样的光芒，抬到嘴边的手颤抖着，像在发高烧。
"你想干什么？"她问。
"我要你，"他声音破碎地说，"我要你来换我父亲的命！"
"你什么意思？"她倒抽一口气。
"彼得·凯恩昨晚杀了我父亲，"他说。
"你疯了，"她惊叫道，"我父亲在这里——你跟我说过。"
"我骗你的。我跟你说过什么有什么关系？彼得·凯恩在押往基镇的路上逃跑了，他回了俱乐部，杀了我父亲！"

## XXX

===Original===
The girl looked at him, speechless.
"It isn't true!" she cried.
"It's not true, isn't it?" Jeffrey almost howled the words. He was mad with hate, with grief, with desire for cruel vengeance. "I'll show you whether it's not true, my lady. You're my wife—do you understand that? If you don't, you're going to."
He flung out of the cell, turning to voice his foul mind, and then the door clanged on her, and he strode out of the hall into the little house that was once the Governor's residence, and which was now the general headquarters of the Big Printer.
He poured himself out a stiff dose of whisky and drank it undiluted, and the man who had accompanied him watched him curiously.

===Chinese===
那女孩看着他，说不出话来。
"这不是真的！"她喊道。
"不是真的，是吗？"杰弗里几乎是嚎叫着说出这句话的。他被仇恨、悲痛和残忍的报复欲折磨得几近疯狂，"我会让你看看是不是真的，我的夫人。你是我的妻子——你明白吗？不明白的话，你会明白的。"
他猛冲出牢房，转身还想吐出些污言秽语，但牢门已经哐当一声在她面前关上了。他大步穿过大厅，走进那座曾经是典狱长住所、如今已是"大印钞师"（the Big Printer）总指挥部的小房子。
他给自己倒了一大杯威士忌，一饮而尽，没有兑水。陪他一起来的那个人好奇地看着他。

===Original===
"Jeff, it looks to me as if it's time to make a getaway. We can't keep these people here very long. The men are scared, too."
"Scared, are they?" sneered Jeffrey Legge. "I guess they'd be more scared if they were in front of a judge and jury."
"That's the kind of scare they're anxious to avoid," said his lieutenant calmly. "Anyway, Jeff, we're getting near the end, and it seems to me that it's the time for all sensible men to find a little home on the other side of the water."
Legge thought for a long time, and when he spoke his voice was more calm.
"Perhaps you're right," he said. "Tell them they can clear tonight."

===Chinese===
"杰夫，我看是时候撤了。这些人我们关不了太久。手底下的人也害怕了。"
"害怕了？"杰弗里·莱格冷笑道，"我猜要是让他们站到法官和陪审团面前，他们会更害怕。"
"他们想避免的正是那种害怕，"他的副手平静地说，"总之，杰夫，快到头了。我觉得该是所有聪明人到水那边去找个小窝安身的时候了。"
莱格沉思了许久，再开口时声音平静了许多。
"也许你说得对，"他说，"告诉他们今晚可以走。"

===Original===
The other man was taken aback by the answer.
"Tonight?" he said. "Well, I don't know that there's that hurry."
"Tell 'em to clear tonight. They've got all the money they want. I'm shutting this down."
"Who killed your father?"
"Peter Kane," snarled Legge. "I've got the full strength of it. The police are hiding him up, but he did the killing all right. They found him on the premises in the morning."
He sat awhile, staring moodily at the glass in his hand.

===Chinese===
对方被这个回答惊到了。
"今晚？"他说，"嗯，我看没那么急吧。"
"告诉他们今晚走。他们想要的钱已经够了。我要关门大吉了。"
"谁杀了你父亲？"
"彼得·凯恩，"莱格恶狠狠地说，"我已经查得一清二楚。警察在包庇他，但人确实是他杀的。第二天早上他们在他自己的地盘上发现了他。"
他坐了一会儿，闷闷不乐地盯着手中的酒杯。

===Original===
"Let them go tonight," he said, "every one of them. I'll tell them myself."
"Do you want me to go?" asked the other.
Legge nodded.
"Yes; I want to be alone. I'm going to fix two people tonight," he said, between his teeth, "and I'm fixing them good."
"Some of the men like Johnny Gray; they were in boob with him," suggested his assistant, but Jeffrey stopped him with an oath.
"That's another reason they can get out," he said, "and they can't know too soon."
He jumped to his feet and strode out of the room, the man following at a distance.

===Chinese===
"让他们今晚走，"他说，"一个不留。我亲自去告诉他们。"
"那你要我走吗？"对方问。
莱格点了点头。
"要；我想一个人待着。今晚我要收拾两个人，"他咬牙切齿地说，"而且要收拾得干干净净。"
"有些人跟约翰尼·格雷关系不错；他们一起蹲过监狱，"他的助手提醒道，但杰弗里一句粗话打断了他。
"这正是他们可以走的另一个理由，"他说，"越快知道越好。"
他一跃而起，大步冲出房间，那人远远地跟在后面。

===Original===
There were two halls to the prison, and it was into the second that he turned. This was brilliantly illuminated. The doors had been removed from most of the cells, and several of them were obviously sleeping-rooms for the half a dozen men who sat about a table playing cards. At only four places were the cell doors intact, for behind these were the delicate printing presses which from morning till night were turning out and numbering French, American and English paper currency. There was not one of the men at the table, or who came to the doors of their cubicles, attracted by the unusual appearance of Legge, who had not served long terms of imprisonment on forgery charges. Jeffrey had recruited them as carefully as a theatrical producer recruits his beauty chorus. They were men without homes, without people, mainly without hope; men inured to the prison system, and who found, in this novel method of living, a delightful variation of the life to which they were most accustomed.

===Chinese===
监狱有两座大厅，他走进了第二座。这里灯火通明。大多数牢房的门已被拆除，其中几间显然是卧室，六个男人围坐在一张桌旁打牌。只有四间牢房的门完好无损，因为这些牢房里面放着精密的印刷机，从早到晚不停地印制和编号法国、美国和英国的纸币。桌旁的人中，或者那些被莱格罕见的出现吸引到隔间门口的人，没有一个不是因伪造罪服过长刑期的。杰弗里招募他们就像戏剧制作人挑选歌舞团演员一样精心。他们是没有家、没有亲人、基本上也没有希望的人；他们习惯了监狱体制，在这种新奇的生活方式中找到了对他们最熟悉生活的愉快调剂。

===Original===
It was believed by the authorities that Keytown Jail was in the hands of a syndicate engaged in experimental work of a highly complicated character, and no obstacle had been placed in the way of laying power cables to the "laboratories." Jeff had found the safest asylum in the land, and one which was more strongly guarded than any he could have built.
His speech was short and to the point.
"Boys, I guess that the time has come when we've got to make the best of our way home. You've all enough money to live comfortably on for the rest of your lives, and I advise you to get out of the country as soon as you can. You have your passports; you know the way; and there's no time like the present."

===Chinese===
当局相信基镇监狱掌握在一个从事高度复杂实验工作的财团手中，因此没有在铺设通往"实验室"的电缆方面设置任何障碍。杰夫找到了全国最安全的庇护所，一个比他自己能建的任何地方都守卫更森严的所在。
他的话简短而切中要害。
"伙计们，我想是时候各回各家了。你们手里的钱够你们舒舒服服过完这辈子了，我建议你们尽快离开这个国家。你们有护照，知道路怎么走；现在正是最好的时机。"

===Original===
"Do you mean that we've got to go tonight, Jeff?" asked a voice.
"I mean tonight. I'll have a car run you into London; but you'll have to leave your kit behind, but you can afford that."
"What are you going to do with the factory?"
"That's my business," said Jeff.
The proposal did not find universal favour, but they stood in such awe of the Big Printer that, though they demurred, they obeyed. By ten o'clock that night the prison was empty, except for Jeffrey and his assistant.

===Chinese===
"你是说我们今晚就得走，杰夫？"有人问。
"我说的就是今晚。我会派车送你们进伦敦；你们的家伙什得留下，不过你们也买得起新的。"
"工厂你打算怎么办？"
"那是我的事，"杰夫说。
这个提议并未获得全体赞同，但他们对"大印钞师"如此敬畏，虽然心存异议，还是服从了。当晚十点，监狱里已空无一人，只剩下杰弗里和他的助手。

===Original===
"I didn't see Bill Holliss go," said the latter; but Jeffrey Legge was too intent upon his plans to give the matter a moment's thought.
"Maybe you'll see yourself go now, Jenkins," said he. "You can take your two-seater and run anywhere you like."
"Let me stay till the morning," asked the man.
"You'll go tonight. Otherwise, what's the use of sending the other fellows away?"

===Chinese===
"我没看见比尔·霍利斯（Bill Holliss）走，"后者说；但杰弗里·莱格全神贯注于自己的计划，顾不上想这件事。
"杰金斯（Jenkins），也许你现在也该走了，"他说，"你可以开你的双座车，爱去哪儿去哪儿。"
"让我待到早上吧，"那人请求道。
"你今晚走。不然把其他人送走还有什么意义？"

===Original===
He closed the big gate upon the car. He was alone with his wife and with the man he hated. He could think calmly now. The madness of rage had passed. He made a search of a little storeroom and found what he was looking for. It was a stout rope. With this over his arm, and a storm-lamp in his hand, he went out into the yard and came to a little shed built against the wall. Unlocking the rusty padlock, he pulled the doors apart. The shed was empty; the floor was inches thick with litter, and, going back, he found a broom and swept it clean. With the aid of a ladder he mounted to a beam that ran transversely across the roof, and fastened one end of the rope securely. Coming down, he spent half an hour in making a noose.

===Chinese===
他关上了通往外面的大门。现在只有他和他的妻子、还有他恨的那个人。他现在可以冷静思考了。疯狂的暴怒已经过去。他在一个小储藏室里搜寻，找到了他要的东西——一根结实的绳子。他把绳子搭在臂弯，提着一盏防风灯，走进院子，来到紧靠围墙搭建的一间小棚屋前。他打开锈迹斑斑的挂锁，拉开门。棚屋是空的；地板上堆积了几英寸厚的垃圾。他回去找来一把扫帚，把地面打扫干净。借助一把梯子，他爬上横跨屋顶的一根横梁，把绳子的一端牢牢系住。下来之后，他花了半个小时编好了一个绳套。

===Original===
He was in the death-house. Under his feet was the fatal trap that a pull of the rusty lever would spring. He wanted to make the experiment, but the trap would take a lot of time to pull up. His face was pouring with perspiration when he had finished. The night was close, and a flicker of lightning illuminated for a second the gloomy recesses of the prison yard.
As he entered the hall a low growl of thunder came to him, but the storm in his heart was more violent than any nature could provide.

===Chinese===
他身处死牢之中。脚下就是那致命的活板门，一拉生锈的操纵杆就会弹开。他想试验一下，但要把活板门重新合上很费功夫。忙完之后，他满脸淌汗。夜晚沉闷，一道闪电一闪而过，照亮了监狱院子阴暗的角落。
他走进大厅时，隐隐传来一阵雷声，但他心中的风暴比自然界能制造的任何风暴都要猛烈。

===Original===
He tiptoed up the iron stairs to the landing, and came at last to No. 4 and hesitated. His enemy could wait. Creeping down the stairs again, his heart beating thunderously, he stood outside the door of the condemned cell. The key trembled as he inserted it in the lock. No sound broke the stillness as the door opened stealthily, and he slipped into the room.
He waited, holding his breath, not knowing whether she were awake or asleep, and then crept forward to the bed. He saw the outline of a figure.
"Marney," he said huskily, groping for her face.
And then two hands like steel clamps caught him by the throat and flung him backward.
"I want you, Jeffrey Legge," said a voice—the voice of Johnny Gray.

===Chinese===
他踮着脚尖走上铁楼梯来到平台，终于走到了4号牢房前，犹豫了。他的敌人可以再等等。他蹑手蹑脚地再次走下楼梯，心如擂鼓，站在了死囚牢房的门前。钥匙插入锁孔时他的手在颤抖。牢门悄无声息地打开了，没有打破一片寂静，他溜进了房间。
他屏住呼吸等着，不知道她是醒着还是睡着，然后蹑手蹑脚地朝床铺走去。他看到一个模糊的人影。
"玛妮，"他沙哑地唤道，伸手去摸她的脸。
紧接着，两只铁钳般的手扼住了他的喉咙，把他猛地向后摔去。
"我找你算账来了，杰弗里·莱格，"一个声音说——那是约翰尼·格雷的声音。

## XXXI

===Original===
Johnny Gray came to consciousness with a violent headache and a sense of suffocating restriction, which he discovered was due to his wing collar holding tightly in spite of the rough usage that had been his. This fact would have been pleasing to Parker, but was intensely discomforting to the wearer, and in a minute he had stripped the offending collar from his throat and had risen unsteadily to his feet.

The room in which he was had a familiar appearance. It was a cell, and﻿

===Chinese===
约翰尼·格雷（Johnny Gray）醒来时头痛欲裂，感觉被紧紧箍着喘不过气来。他发现那是因为他的翼领依然紧紧扣着，尽管之前受了不少粗暴对待。这个事实要是让帕克（Parker）知道了大概会很高兴，但对戴的人来说却难受至极。不到一分钟，他便把那碍事的领子从脖子上扯了下来，摇摇晃晃地站了起来。

他所在的房间有一种熟悉的外观。这是一间牢房，而且——

===Original===
Keytown Jail! He remembered Fenner's warning. So Fenner knew! Keytown Jail, sold by the Government to﻿—Jeffrey Legge! The idea was preposterous; but why not? A timber merchant had bought a jail at Hereford; a firm of caterers had purchased an old prison in the North of England, and were serving afternoon teas in the cells.

Now he understood. Keytown Prison was the headquarters of the Big Printer. The one place in the world that the police would never dream of searching, particularly if, as he guessed, Jeffrey Legge had offered some specious excuse for his presence and the presence of his company in this isolated part of the world.

===Chinese===
基镇监狱（Keytown Jail）！他想起了芬纳（Fenner）的警告。原来芬纳知道！基镇监狱，被政府卖给了——杰弗里·莱格（Jeffrey Legge）！这念头荒唐透顶；但为什么不呢？有个木材商在赫里福德（Hereford）买了一座监狱；一家餐饮公司在英格兰北部买了一座旧监狱，现在在牢房里供应下午茶了。

他现在明白了。基镇监狱就是「大印钞师」（the Big Printer）的总部。这是全世界最不可能被警察搜查的地方，尤其是如他所推测的那样，杰弗里·莱格为他本人及其公司出现在这个偏远地区提供了某种似是而非的借口。

===Original===
The sound of voices came faintly up to him, and he heard a door bang and the clicking of locks; and with that sound he recalled the happenings of the evening. It must be Peter: they had got him too. In spite of his discomfiture, in spite of the awful danger in which he knew he was, he laughed softly to himself.

Above his bed was a window with scarcely a whole pane. But there was no escape that way. A thought struck him, and, leaning down, he tapped a Morse message on the floor. If it was Peter, he could understand. He heard the answering tap which came feebly, and when he signalled again he knew that whoever was in the cell below had no knowledge of the Morse code. He searched his pockets and found a tiny scrap of pencil, but could find no paper, except a bundle of five-pound notes, which his captors had not troubled to remove. Here was both stationery and the means of writing, but how could he communicate with the occupant of the cell below? Presently a plan suggested itself, and he tore off the lapel of his dinner-jacket and unravelled the silk. Tying the pencil to the end to give it weight, he slowly lowered his message, hoping, though it seemed unlikely, that his fellow-prisoner would be able to see the paper.

===Chinese===
微弱的说话声从上面传来，他听到一扇门砰地关上，还有锁头咔嗒作响；那声音让他想起了今晚发生的事。一定是彼得：他们也把他抓来了。尽管处境狼狈，尽管深知自己身处极大的危险之中，他还是轻轻地笑了一声。

他床上方有一扇窗户，几乎没有一块完整的玻璃。但那条路走不通。他突然想到一个办法，俯身在地板上敲出一段摩尔斯电码。如果是彼得，他应该能懂。他听到回应的敲击声传来，但很微弱。他再次发信号，便知道牢房下面的人不懂摩尔斯电码。他翻遍口袋，找到一小截铅笔，却找不到纸，只有一捆五英镑的钞票——抓他的人懒得拿走。文具和书写工具都有了，但怎么跟下面牢房里的人联系呢？很快他想到了一个办法，撕下晚礼服的翻领，拆开缝线。他把铅笔绑在丝绸末端增加重量，慢慢把信息放了下去，尽管希望渺茫，他仍期待他的难友能看到那张纸。

===Original===
To his joy he felt a tug, and when, a few minutes later, he carefully drew up the message, it was to find, written underneath his own, one which left him white and shaking.

Marney here! He groaned aloud at the thought. It was too light now to risk any further communication. There was a ewer of water and a basin in the cell, and with this he relieved the aching in his head; and when breakfast came, he was ready.

===Chinese===
令他欣喜的是，他感到了一下拉扯。几分钟后，他小心翼翼地把信息拉上来，发现在他写的字下面，还有一行字，那行字让他面色煞白、浑身发抖。

玛妮（Marney）也在这里！想到这个，他痛苦地呻吟了一声。天已经亮了，不能再冒险继续传递消息了。牢房里有一个水罐和一个脸盆，他用这些水缓解了头痛。早饭送来时，他已准备好应对。

===Original===
The man who brought in the tray was a stranger to him, as also was the man who stood on guard at the door, revolver in hand.

"What's the great idea?" asked Johnny coolly, sitting on the bed and swinging his legs. "Has Jeff bought a jail to practise in? Wouldn't it have been cheaper to have gone over the Alps?"

"You shut up, Johnny Gray," growled the man. "You'll be sorry for yourself before you're out of here."

"Who isn't?" asked Johnny. "How is Peter?"

===Chinese===
端餐盘进来的人他不认识，门口守卫的那人他也不认识，那人手里握着一把左轮手枪。

"这出戏是什么意思？"约翰尼冷静地问道，坐在床上晃着腿。"杰夫买了座监狱来练手？翻越阿尔卑斯山不是更便宜吗？"

"你闭嘴，约翰尼·格雷，"那人吼道。"等你出了这里，有你后悔的。"

"谁不后悔呢？"约翰尼问道。"彼得怎么样了？"

===Original===
"You know damned well Peter has escaped," said the other before he could check himself.

"Escaped!" said the delighted Johnny. "You don't mean that?"

"Never mind what I mean," growled the man, realising he had said too much. "You keep a civil tongue in your head, Gray, and you'll be treated square. If you don't, there are plenty of men on the spot to make Dartmoor a paradise compared with Keytown."

===Chinese===
"你他妈的清楚得很，彼得已经逃了，"那人脱口而出，还没来得及收住。

"逃了！"约翰尼高兴地说。"你是说真的？"

"我什么意思你别管，"那人吼道，意识到自己说多了。"你说话放尊重点，格雷，我们会公事公办。你要是不识相，这里有的是人手，能把达特穆尔（Dartmoor）变成跟基镇比起来的天堂。"

===Original===
The door slammed in Johnny Gray's face, but he was so absorbed in the news which the man had unwillingly given to him that he had to force himself to eat.

Soon after the man came to take away the tray.

"What's your name, bo', anyway?" said Johnny carelessly. "I hate calling you 'face'﻿—it's low."

"Bill's my name," said the man, "and you needn't call me Bill either. You say 'sir' to me."

"Woof!" said Johnny admiringly. "You're talking like a real screw!"

The door slammed in his face. He had further time to consider his plans. They had taken away his watch and chain, his gold cigarette-case and the small penknife he carried, but these losses did not worry him in the slightest. His chief anxiety was to know the exact character of Keytown Prison. And that he determined to learn at the earliest opportunity.

===Chinese===
门砰地一声在约翰尼·格雷面前关上了，但他完全沉浸在那人不情愿透露给他的消息中，以至于不得不强迫自己吃东西。

不久那人又来收走餐盘。

"对了，你叫什么名字，伙计？"约翰尼漫不经心地问。"我讨厌叫你'脸'——太低俗了。"

"我叫比尔（Bill），"那人说，"你也不必叫我比尔。你得叫我'先生'。"

"好家伙！"约翰尼赞叹道。"你说话真像个正经狱警（screw）！"

门又砰地关上了。他有更多时间考虑他的计划。他们拿走了他的手表和表链、金质烟盒和他随身携带的小折刀，但这些损失他一点都不在乎。他最想知道的是基镇监狱的确切情况。他决定一有机会就去摸清。

===Original===
It was late in the afternoon; he guessed it was somewhere in the neighbourhood of four when his lunch came, and he was quite ready to eat it, though a little suspicious of its possible accessories.

"No poison in this, Bill?" he asked pleasantly as he took the bread and cheese from the man's hand.

"There's no need to poison you; we could starve you, couldn't we?" said the other. "If Jeff was here, maybe I'd get a rapping for giving you anything."

"Gone away, has he? Well, prisons are more pleasant when the governor's away. Am I right, Bill? Now, what do you say to a couple of hundred of real money?"

"For what?" asked the man, stopping at the door. "If you mean it's for letting you make a getaway, why, you're silly! You're going to stay here till Jeff fixes you."

===Chinese===
已经是下午很晚了；他猜大约四点左右午饭送来了，他完全准备好吃，虽然对食物可能被动了手脚有些疑虑。

"这里面没下毒吧，比尔？"他愉快地问道，从那人手里接过面包和奶酪。

"没必要毒死你；我们可以饿你，不是吗？"那人说。"要是杰夫在这儿，我给你吃东西说不定还要挨骂呢。"

"他走了？嗯，典狱长不在的时候监狱总是舒服些。我说得对吧，比尔？那么，你对两百块真钞票怎么看？"

"买什么？"那人停下脚步问道。"你要是想让我放你逃跑，那你是痴心妄想！你得在这儿待到杰夫处置你。"

===Original===
All the day Johnny had heard, or rather felt, a peculiar whirr of sound coming from some remote quarter of the prison.

"Got electric light here, Bill?" he said conversationally.

"Yes, we have," said the other. "This is a model boob, this is."

"I'll bet it is," said Johnny grimly. "Are you running any electric radiators in my cell tonight, or do you want all the power for the press?"

He saw the man's face twitch.

"Of course, you're running the slush factory here﻿—everybody knows that. Take my advice. Bill﻿—go whilst the going's good. Or the bulls will have you inside the realest boob you've seen."

===Chinese===
一整天约翰尼都在听到——或者说感觉到——从监狱某个远处传来的奇特嗡嗡声。

"这里有电灯吗，比尔？"他闲聊似的问道。

"有，"那人说。"这是模范监狱（boob），这个。"

"我敢打赌是的，"约翰尼阴沉地说。"今晚你在我牢房里开电暖器吗，还是把所有的电都给印刷机了？"

他看到那人的脸抽搐了一下。

"当然，你们在这里开伪钞（slush）工厂——谁都知道。听我一句劝，比尔——趁早走人。否则警察（bulls）会把你塞进你见过的最货真价实的监狱里。"

===Original===
He had made the guard more than a little uncomfortable, as he saw, and sought to press home the impression he had created.

"Jeffrey's going to shop you sooner or later, because he's a natural born shopper. And he's got the money, Bill, to get away with, and the motorcars and aeroplanes. You haven't got that. You'll have to walk on your own pads. And the bulls will get you halfway over the field."

"Oh, shut up!" said the man uncomfortably, and the conversation ended, as in the morning, with the slamming of the door.

===Chinese===
他看到自己让那个守卫相当不自在，便趁热打铁，把印象再加深几分。

"杰弗里迟早会出卖（shop）你，因为他天生就是个出卖人的人。他有钱跑路，比尔，有机动车和飞机。你没有。你只能靠自己的脚跑。而且警察会在半路上就把你抓住。"

"哦，闭嘴！"那人不舒服地说。谈话就此结束，跟早上一样，以摔门告终。

===Original===
Presently a little spyhole in the cell door opened.

"What made you think this is a print-shop?" asked Bill's voice.

"I don't think anything about it; I know," said Johnny decisively. "If you like to come to me this evening I'll tell you the name of every worker here, the position of every press, and the length of the lagging you'll get."

The cover of the spyhole dropped.

Jeffrey was away; that was all to the good. If he remained away for the whole of the night﻿ ﻿… He was worried about Marney, and it required all his strength of state of nerves.

===Chinese===
不一会儿，牢房门上的小窥视孔打开了。

"你怎么想到这是个印刷作坊的？"比尔的声音问道。

"我不是想到的；我知道，"约翰尼断然说道。"你要是今晚来找我，我可以告诉你这里每个工人的名字、每台印刷机的位置，还有你将被判处的刑期。"

窥视孔的盖子落下了。

杰弗里不在；这再好不过了。要是他整晚都不回来……他担心玛妮，他用尽全部意志力才没有让自己陷入焦躁不安的状态。

===Original===
In an hour Bill returned, and this time he brought no guard but himself, but, for safety's sake, carried on his conversation through a little grille in the door.

"You're bluffing, Johnny Gray. We've got a fellow here who was in boob with you, and he says you're the biggest bluffer that ever lived. You don't know anything."

"I know almost everything," said Johnny immodestly. "For instance, I know you've got a young lady in the cell below. How's she doing?"

The man was taken aback for a moment.

"Who told you?" he asked suspiciously. "Nobody else has been here, have they?"

"Nobody at all. It is part of my general knowledge. Now listen, Bill. How are you treating that lady? And your life hangs on your answer﻿—don't forget it."

===Chinese===
一小时后比尔回来了，这次他没有带守卫，只身一人，但出于安全考虑，他通过门上的小铁栅栏进行对话。

"你在虚张声势，约翰尼·格雷。我们这儿有个跟你一起蹲过监狱的伙计，他说你是有史以来最大的虚张声势者。你什么都不知道。"

"我几乎什么都知道，"约翰尼毫不谦虚地说。"比如我知道你们在下面的牢房里关了一位年轻女士。她怎么样？"

那人一时愣住了。

"谁告诉你的？"他疑心重重地问。"没有别人来过，对吧？"

"一个人都没有。这是我的常识。现在听好了，比尔。你对那位女士怎么样？你的命就悬在你的回答上——别忘了。"

===Original===
"She's all right," said Bill casually. "They've given her the condemned cell, with a bathroom and all, and a proper bed﻿—not like yours. And you can't scare me, Gray."

"I'll bet I can't," said Johnny. "Bring me some water."

But the water was not forthcoming, and it was dark before the man made his reappearance. Johnny listened at the door; he was coming alone. Johnny pulled up the leg of his trousers and showed those suspenders which were Parker's pride. But they were not ordinary suspenders. Strapped to the inside of the calf was a small holster. The automatic it carried was less than four inches in length, but its little blunt-nosed bullets were man-stoppers of a particularly deadly kind.

===Chinese===
"她很好，"比尔漫不经心地说。"他们给她安排了死囚牢，带浴室什么的，还有张像样的床——不像你的。你吓不住我的，格雷。"

"我敢打赌吓不住，"约翰尼说。"给我拿点水来。"

但水没有送来，那人再次出现时天已经黑了。约翰尼在门边听着；他一个人来的。约翰尼卷起裤腿，露出那条让帕克引以为豪的吊带裤。但那不是普通的吊带裤。绑在小腿内侧的是一个小枪套。里面那把自动手枪不到四英寸长，但它的钝头小子弹是特别致命的止人弹。

===Original===
The door swung open, and Bill stepped in.

"Jeff's back﻿—" he began, and then:

"Step in, and step lively," said Johnny.

His arm had shot out, and the pistol hand of the jailer was pinned to his side.

"This gun may look pretty paltry, but it would blow a square inch out of your heart, and that's enough to seriously inconvenience you for the remainder of your short life."

With a turn of his wrist he wrenched the revolver from the man's grasp.

"Sit over there," he said. "Is anybody in the hall?"

"For God's sake, don't let Jeff see you. He'll kill me," pleaded the agitated prisoner.

"I'd hate for him to do that," said Johnny.

===Chinese===
门开了，比尔走了进来。

"杰夫回来了——"他刚开口，紧接着：

"进来，动作快点，"约翰尼说。

他的手臂闪电般伸出，狱警握枪的手被紧紧按在他身侧。

"这把枪看起来可能很不起眼，但它能在你的心脏上轰出一平方英寸的洞，那足够让你短暂的余生非常不舒服了。"

他手腕一拧，把左轮手枪从那人手里夺了过来。

"坐那边去，"他说。"大厅里有人吗？"

"看在上帝的份上，别让杰夫看见你。他会杀了我的，"那个惊慌失措的囚犯哀求道。

"我可不想让他那么做，"约翰尼说。

===Original===
He peeped out into the hall: it was empty, and he went back to his prisoner. "Stand against the wall. I'm going to give you the twice-over."

His hands searched quickly but effectively. The key he was putting in his pocket when he noticed the design of the ward.

"Passkey, I fancy. Now, don't make a fuss, Bill, because you'll be let out first thing in the morning, and maybe I'll have a good word to say for you at the Oxford Assizes. There's something about you that I like. Give me the simple criminal, and the Lord knows you're simple enough!"

He stepped out of the cell, snapped the lock of the door, and, keeping in the shadow, walked swiftly along the gallery until he came to the open stairway on to the floor below.

===Chinese===
他朝大厅里窥了一眼：空无一人，他回到囚犯面前。"靠墙站着。我要搜你的身。"

他的双手迅速而有效地搜了一遍。他把钥匙放进口袋时，注意到了钥匙齿的形状。

"万能钥匙，我猜。好了，别闹了，比尔，因为你明天一早就会被放出来，而且也许我还会在牛津巡回法庭（Oxford Assizes）上替你说句好话。你这人有我喜欢的地方。我就喜欢老实的罪犯，上帝知道你够老实的了！"

他走出牢房，咔嗒一声锁上门，沿着走廊贴着阴影快步走去，直到来到通往下一层的楼梯口。

===Original===
The hall was untenanted. Apparently Bill was the only jailer. He had reached the floor when the door at the end of the hall opened and somebody came in. He flattened himself in one of the recessed cell doorways. Two men entered, and one, he guessed, was Jeff. One, two, three, four﻿—the fourth door from the end. That was Marney's door, immediately under his own. He saw Jeffrey stop, heard the too-familiar grind of the lock, and his enemy disappeared, leaving the second man on guard outside.

If Jeffrey had made an attempt to close the door behind him, Johnny would have shot down the guard and taken the consequences. But the man was absent for only a few minutes. When he came out, he was shouting incoherently threats that made the hair rise on Johnny Gray's neck. But they were only threats.

===Chinese===
大厅里没有人。显然比尔是唯一的狱警。他刚到下一层，大厅尽头的门就开了，有人走了进来。他贴在一扇凹进去的牢房门廊里。两个人进来了，其中一个，他猜就是杰夫。一、二、三、四——从尽头数第四个门。那是玛妮的门，就在他自己房间的正下方。他看到杰弗里停了下来，听到那熟悉得令人厌恶的锁头转动声，然后他的敌人消失了，留下第二个人在门外站岗。

如果杰弗里试图关门，约翰尼会毫不犹豫地开枪击倒那个守卫，承担一切后果。但那人只离开了几分钟。当他出来时，正语无伦次地大声威胁着什么，那些话让约翰尼·格雷的汗毛都竖了起来。但那不过是威胁罢了。

===Original===
The hall door closed on Jeffrey Legge and Johnny moved swiftly to No. 4. As the door opened, the girl shrank back against the wall.

"Don't touch me!" she cried.

"Marney!"

At the sound of his voice she stood, rooted to the spot. The next second she was laughing and weeping in his arms.

"But, Johnny, how did you get here?﻿ ﻿… where were you?﻿ ﻿… you won't leave me?"

He soothed her and quietened her as only Johnny Gray could.

"I'll stay﻿ ﻿… I think this fellow will come back. If he does, he will wish he hadn't!"

And Jeffrey came. As the grip of strong hands closed on his throat, and the hateful voice of his enemy came to his ears, Johnny's prophecy was justified.

===Chinese===
大厅的门在杰弗里·莱格身后关上，约翰尼迅速向4号牢房移动。门一打开，那女孩就缩回墙角。

"别碰我！"她喊道。

"玛妮！"

听到他的声音，她愣在原地。下一秒，她便在他怀里又哭又笑。

"可是，约翰尼，你怎么到这儿来的？……你之前在哪儿？……你不会离开我吧？"

他像只有约翰尼·格雷能做到的那样安慰她、让她平静下来。

"我会留下……我觉得那个家伙会回来的。他要是回来，他会后悔的！"

杰弗里果然来了。当一双有力的手掐住他的喉咙，当他敌人的声音传入耳中时，约翰尼的预言应验了。

## XXXII / 第三十二章

===Original===
For a second Legge was paralysed with rage and fear. Then, in the wildness of his despair, he kicked at the man, who had slipped from the bed and was holding him. He heard an exclamation, felt for a second the fingers relax; and, slipping like an eel from the grasp, flew to the door and closed it. He stood, breathless and panting by the doorway, until he heard the sound of steel against the inner keyhole, and in a flash realised that Johnny had secured the passkey. Quick as lightning, he slipped his own key back into the lock and turned it slightly, so that it could not be pushed out from the other side.

===Chinese===
莱格（Jeffrey Legge）先是被愤怒和恐惧惊得呆了一瞬。随即，在绝望的狂乱中，他朝那个人猛踢过去——那人已经从床上滑下来，正抓着他。他听到一声惊呼，感觉到那抓握的手指松了一瞬；他像鳗鱼一样从对方手中挣脱，飞身扑向门口，把门关上。他靠在门框上气喘吁吁，直到听见钢钥匙插进内侧锁孔的声音，这才猛然醒悟——约翰尼（Johnny Gray）已经拿到了万能钥匙。他闪电般地把自己的钥匙插回锁孔，轻轻一转，让另一边的人推不出来。

===Original===
Johnny Gray! How had he got there? He fled up the stairs and hammered on the door of the cell where he thought his prisoner was held safe. A surly voice replied to him.

"You swine!" he howled. "You let him go! You twister! You can stay there and starve, damn you!"

"I didn't let him go. He held me up. Look out, Jeff, he's got a gun."

===Chinese===
约翰尼·格雷！他怎么到那儿去的？他冲上楼梯，使劲锤他认为关着俘虏的牢房门。一个粗暴的声音回了他。

"你这混账！"他咆哮道，"你放了他！你这叛徒！你就待在那儿饿死吧，该死的！"

"我没放他。他拿枪逼住了我。小心，杰弗里，他有枪。"

===Original===
The news staggered the man. The search of Johnny's clothing had been of a perfunctory nature, but he had thought that it was impossible that any kind of weapon could have been concealed.

"Let me out, guv'nor," pleaded the prisoner. "You've got a key."

There was a third key in his house, Jeffrey remembered. Perhaps this man might be of use to him. He was still weak from his wound, and would need assistance.

"All right, I'll get the key. But if you shopped me—"

"I didn't shop you, I tell you. He held me up—"

===Chinese===
这个消息把莱格震住了。搜约翰尼的衣服不过是例行公事，但他本以为绝不可能藏住任何武器。

"放我出去，老板，"牢房里的囚犯哀求道，"你有钥匙。"

杰弗里想起来，自己手头确实还有第三把钥匙。也许这个人能派上用场。他伤口未愈，身子还虚，需要帮手。

"行，我去拿钥匙。但你要是告了密——"

"我没告密，我跟你说。他拿枪逼着我——"

===Original===
Legge went back to his room, found the key, and, taking another stiff dose of whisky, returned and released his man.

"He's got my gun, too," explained Bill. "Where are all the fellows? We'll soon settle with him."

"They've gone," said Jeffrey.

What a fool he had been! If he had had the sense to keep the gang together only for a few hours—But he was safe, unless Johnny found a means of getting through the window.

===Chinese===
莱格回到自己房间，找到钥匙，又灌了一大口威士忌，回来放出了那个人。

"他把我的枪也拿走了，"比尔（Bill）解释道，"其他弟兄呢？咱们很快就能收拾他。"

"都走了，"杰弗里说。

他真是个蠢货！要是他有点脑子，让那帮人多待几个小时——不过他还是安全的，除非约翰尼能找到办法从窗户出去。

===Original===
"In my room you'll find a pistol; it is in the top right-hand corner of my desk," he said quickly. "Take it and get outside Johnny's cell—on the yard side. If he tries to escape that way, shoot. Because, if he escapes, you're going a long journey, my friend."

Inside the cell, a chagrined Johnny Gray sat down on the girl's bed to consider the possibilities of the position.

"My dear, there's going to be serious trouble here, and I don't want you to think otherwise," he said. "I should imagine there were quite a number of men in this prison, in which case, though I shall probably get two or three of them, they'll certainly get me in the end."

She sat by his side, holding his hand, and the pressure of her fingers was eloquent of the faith she had in him.

"Johnny, dear, does it matter very much what happens now? They can't come in, and we can't get out. How long will it take to starve us to death?"

===Chinese===
"我房间里有一把手枪；在我书桌右上角，"他急促地说，"拿着它，守在约翰尼的牢房外面——院子那一侧。他要是想从那边逃，就开枪。因为他要是跑了，你也别想有好日子过，我的朋友。"

牢房里，懊恼的约翰尼·格雷在姑娘的床上坐下，盘算着眼前的局面。

"亲爱的，这里要出大事了，我不希望你想得太轻松，"他说，"这个监狱里恐怕有不少人，那样的话，就算我能放倒两三个，他们最终还是会抓住我。"

她坐在他身旁，握着他的手，指尖的力度诉说着她对他的信任。

"约翰尼，亲爱的，现在发生什么真的那么重要吗？他们进不来，我们也出不去。把我们饿死要多久？"

===Original===
Johnny had already considered that problem.

"About three days," he said, in such a matter-of-fact tone that she laughed. "My only hope, Marney, is that your father, who, as I told you, has escaped, may know more about this place than he has admitted."

"Did you know anything about it?" she asked.

He hesitated.

"Yes, I think I did. I wasn't sure, though I was a fool not to locate it just as soon as Fenner warned me against Keytown Jail. These chaps like to speak in parables, and mystery is as the breath of their nostrils. Besides, I should have been certain that Fenner knew the jail had been taken over from the Government."

===Chinese===
约翰尼早就想过这个问题。

"大约三天，"他说，语气平淡得让她笑了出来。"我唯一的希望，玛妮，是你父亲——我跟你说过他已经逃出去了——对这个地方的了解可能比他承认的要多。"

"你自己知道什么吗？"她问。

他犹豫了一下。

"知道，我想我是知道的。虽然我不确定，不过我真蠢，芬纳（Fenner）一警告我别去基镇监狱（Keytown Jail）的时候，就该查清楚。这帮人说话总爱拐弯抹角，神秘兮兮的是他们的命根子。再说了，我本该想到芬纳肯定知道那座监狱已经从政府手里转出去了。"

===Original===
He made a careful examination of the bars about the window, but without instruments or tools to force them, he knew that escape that way was impossible. When, in the early hours of the morning, he saw the patient figure of Bill, he realised the extent of the impossibility.

"Good morning, William. I see you're out," he greeted the scowling sentry, who immediately jumped to cover, flourishing his long-barrelled weapon.

"Don't you show your nose, or I'll blow it off," he threatened. "We've got you, Mr. Gray."

"They've got you, alas, my poor William," said Johnny sadly. "The busies will be here at nine o'clock—you don't suppose that I should have let myself come into a trap like this? Of course, I didn't. I squeaked! It was my only chance, William. And your only chance is to sneak away at the earliest opportunity, and turn State's evidence. I'm addressing you as a friend."

===Chinese===
他仔细检查了窗上的铁栏，但没有工具可以撬开它们，他知道从这儿逃出去是不可能的。清晨时分，他看到比尔那耐心守候的身影，这才彻底意识到逃跑有多不可能。

"早上好，威廉。看来你出来了，"他向那阴沉的岗哨打招呼。那人立刻跳到掩体后面，挥舞着他那长管武器。

"你敢探出脑袋，我就把它轰掉，"他威胁道，"你跑不掉了，格雷先生。"

"跑不掉的是你们，唉，可怜的威廉，"约翰尼哀伤地说，"条子（busies）九点钟就到——你以为我会让自己钻进这种陷阱吗？当然不会。我告了密（squeaked）！这是我唯一的机会，威廉。而你唯一的机会就是尽早溜走，转做污点证人（State's evidence）。我是拿你当朋友才跟你说这番话的。"

===Original===
"You'll never get away from here alive," said the man. "Jeff's going to fix you."

"Indeed?" the prisoner began politely, when a scream made him turn.

"Johnny!"

The shutter which hid the grille in the door was swung back, and the muzzle of Jeffrey's Browning had been pushed through one of the openings. As Johnny dropped flat on the bed, he was stunned by the deafening sound of an explosion. Something hit the wall, ricochetted to the roof, and fell almost at the girl's feet. Before the pistol could be withdrawn, Johnny Gray had fired. A jagged end of iron showed where his bullet struck.

===Chinese===
"你活着出不去的，"那人说，"杰弗里会收拾你。"

"是吗？"囚犯客客气气地刚开了口，一声尖叫让他转过身去。

"约翰尼！"

遮挡牢门铁栅的挡板被掀开了，杰弗里的勃朗宁手枪枪口已经从一个格子里伸了进来。约翰尼扑倒在床上，一声震耳欲聋的爆炸声几乎把他震懵。有什么东西打在墙上，弹到屋顶，落在姑娘脚边。手枪还没来得及抽回去，约翰尼·格雷已经开了一枪。铁栅上露出一个参差不齐的弹痕。

===Original===
"The time for persiflage," said Johnny cheerfully, "is past. Now you will sit in that corner, young lady, and will not budge without permission." He pointed to the wall nearest the door, which afforded perfect cover, and, dragging up a stool, he seated himself by her side. "Jeffrey's got quite a tough proposition," he said in his conversational tone. "He can't burn the prison, because there's nothing to burn. He can't come in, and he mustn't go out. If he would only for one moment take away that infernal key—"

"There is another door going out from the bathroom," she said suddenly. "I think it leads to an exercise ground. You can just see a little railed-off space through the window."

===Chinese===
"说笑的时间，"约翰尼轻快地说，"已经过了。现在你坐到那个角落去，小姐，没我允许不许动。"他指着离门最近的那面墙，那里掩护最好，然后拖过一把凳子，在她身旁坐下。"杰弗里这回可碰上硬茬了，"他用闲聊的口吻说道，"他烧不了监狱，因为没什么可烧的。他进不来，也不能出去。他只要把那把该死的钥匙拿开哪怕一秒钟——"

"浴室还有另一扇门通出去，"她忽然说，"我想是通到放风场的。透过窗户能看见一小块围栏隔开的空地。"

===Original===
Johnny went into the bathroom and examined the door. Screwing his head, he could see, through a broken pane, ten square yards of space, where in olden times a condemned prisoner took his exercise, removed from the gaze of his fellows. He tried the key, and to his delight, it turned. Another minute and he was in the little paved yard.

Looking round, he saw a high and narrow gateway, which seemed to be the only exit from the courtyard. And on the other side of that gateway was William, the sentry, well armed and sufficiently terrified to be dangerous. Slipping off his boots, Johnny crept to the gate and listened. The sound of the man's footsteps pacing the flagged walk came to him. Stooping, he squinted through the keyhole, and saw Bill standing, his back toward him, some six yards away. There was no time to be lost. He inserted the key, and the gate was opened before the man could turn to face the levelled revolver.

===Chinese===
约翰尼走进浴室检查那扇门。歪过头，他透过一块碎玻璃看见了大约十平方码的空地——旧时死囚就是在这块与世隔绝的地方放风的。他试了试钥匙，惊喜地发现它能转动。再过一分钟，他就站在了那片铺石小院里。

环顾四周，他看到一道高窄的门洞，似乎是这个院子唯一的出口。门洞的另一边站着岗哨威廉，全副武装，足够害怕到铤而走险。约翰尼脱掉靴子，蹑手蹑脚地凑到门边倾听。那人踱步踩在石板路上的脚步声传了过来。他弯下腰，眯着眼从锁孔望过去，看见比尔背对着他站在大约六码开外。时间紧迫。他插入钥匙，在那人转身面对瞄准的左轮手枪之前，门已经打开了。

===Original===
"Don't shout," whispered Johnny. "You're either discreet or dead. Hand over that gun, you unfortunate man." He moved swiftly toward the terrified criminal, and relieved him of his weapon.

With a gesture, Johnny directed him to the exercise yard.

"Get in and stay," he said, and locked the door, and for the second time, Bill (his other name, Johnny never discovered, was Holliss) was a prisoner.

===Chinese===
"别嚷，"约翰尼低声说，"你要么识相，要么死。把枪交出来，你这倒霉蛋。"他迅速走向那个吓坏了的罪犯，缴了他的武器。

约翰尼用手势示意他进放风场。

"进去待着，"他说着锁上了门。比尔（他的另一个姓氏，约翰尼始终没有发现，是霍利斯（Holliss））第二次成了囚犯。

===Original===
Skirting the building, he came to the entrance of the hall. The door was open, and with his hand on the uplifted hammer of the gun, and his finger pressing the trigger, Johnny leapt into the building.

"Hands up!" he shouted.

At the words, Jeffrey Legge spun round. There was a boom of sound, something whistled past Gray's face, and he fired twice. But now the man was running, zigzagging to left and right, and Johnny hesitated to fire. He disappeared through the door at the farther end of the hall, shutting it behind him, and Johnny raced after him.

===Chinese===
他沿着建筑边缘摸到大厅入口。门敞开着，约翰尼手扣扳机、击锤高举，纵身跃入楼内。

"举起手来！"他大喝一声。

话音未落，杰弗里·莱格猛地转身。一声轰响，有什么东西呼啸着掠过格雷的脸颊，他连开两枪。但那人已经在跑了，左躲右闪，约翰尼犹豫着没有再开枪。那人消失在大厅尽头的门后，随手把门关上，约翰尼紧追不舍。

===Original===
He was in the courtyard now, facing the grille-covered archway. As he came into view, Jeffrey disappeared through the lodge-keeper's door. Johnny tried the grille, but in vain, for a passkey operates on all locks save the lock of the entrance gate of a prison. That alone is distinct, and may not be opened save by the key that was cut for it.

Covering the lodge-keeper's door with his gun, Johnny waited, and, waiting, heard a rumbling sound. Something was coming down the centre of the archway. The straight line of it came lower and lower. A hanging gate! He had forgotten that most old country prisons were so equipped. Under the cover of this ancient portcullis, Legge could escape, for it masked the entrance of the lodge.

===Chinese===
他现在站在院子里，面对着铁栅封顶的拱门。杰弗里一看到他出现，就从门房的门里消失了。约翰尼试了试铁栅，但无济于事——万能钥匙能打开所有锁，唯独打不开监狱的大门锁。那是唯一与众不同的锁，只有专门配的钥匙才能打开。

约翰尼用枪指着门房的门等待着。等的时候，他听到一阵隆隆声。有东西正从拱门正中降下来。那条笔直的线条越来越低。吊闸！他忘了大多数老式的乡村监狱都装有这种东西。在这道古老吊闸的掩护下，莱格可以逃走，因为它恰好遮住了门房的入口。

===Original===
He turned back to the girl.

"Keep out of sight. He's got away," he warned her. "This fellow isn't finished yet."

The gate was down… Jeffrey put on the overcoat he had left in the lodge, slipped his pistol into his pocket and opened the great gates. He had at least a dozen hours' start, he thought, as he stepped into the open…

"Please do not put your hand in your pocket, Mr. Jeffrey," said a plaintive voice. "I should so hate to shoot a fellow-creature. It would be a deed utterly repugnant to my finest feelings."

Jeffrey raised his hands to their fullest extent, for Mr. Reeder was not alone. Behind him were four armed policemen, a cordon of mounted constabulary, spread in a semicircle, cutting off all avenues of escape. And, most ominous of all, was the deadly scrutiny of Peter Kane, who stood at Reeder's right hand.

===Chinese===
他转身回到姑娘身边。

"别露面。他跑了，"他提醒她，"这家伙还没完。"

吊闸落下了……杰弗里穿上留在门房里的大衣，把手枪塞进口袋，打开了大门。他至少领先了十二个小时，他想，一边跨出门去……

"请不要把手伸进口袋，杰弗里先生（Mr. Jeffrey），"一个哀伤的声音说道，"我实在不忍心对同胞开枪。那将是一件与我最美好的感情全然相悖的事。"

杰弗里把手高高举起，举到了极限——因为里德先生（Mr. J. G. Reeder）并非独自一人。他身后是四名武装警察，一队骑警呈半圆形展开，切断了所有逃路。而最不祥的，是彼得·凯恩（Peter Kane）那死一般的审视——他就站在里德先生的右手边。

## XXXIII

===Original===
For the first time Jeffrey Legge felt the cold contact of handcuffs. He was led back to the porter's lodge, whilst two of the policemen worked at the windlass that raised the hanging gate.

===Chinese===
杰弗里·莱格（Jeffrey Legge）生平第一次感受到了冰冷的手铐。他被押回门房，两个警察转动绞盘，升起了那扇吊门。

===Original===
"It's a cop, Craig," he said, for the inspector in charge was that redoubtable thief-catcher. "But I'm going to squeak all I know. Johnny Gray is in this. He's been working my slush for years. You'll find the presses in the second hall, but the other birds have done some quick flying."

===Chinese===
"认栽了，克雷格（Craig），"他说，因为负责的正是那位令人生畏的侦探，"不过我要把我所知道的全部供出来。约翰尼·格雷（Johnny Gray）也有份。他帮我印伪钞（slush）好几年了。印刷机在第二个厅里，不过其他同伙跑得倒快。"

===Original===
"They've all flown into the police station at Oxford," said Craig, "and they're singing their pretty little songs merrily. The Oxford police took a whole carload of them about eleven o'clock last night. Unfortunately, they weren't so ready to squeak as you."

===Chinese===
"他们全都飞到牛津（Oxford）警察局去了，"克雷格说，"正高高兴兴地唱着他们的小曲儿呢。牛津警方昨晚十一点左右一车拉走了他们一整窝。可惜他们没有你这么痛快。"

===Original===
"Johnny Gray's in it, I tell you."

===Chinese===
"我告诉你，约翰尼·格雷也卷在里面。"

===Original===
"Oh, how can you say such a thing?" said the shocked Mr. Reeder. "I'm perfectly sure Mr. Gray is quite innocent."

===Chinese===
"哦，你怎么能说这种话？"里德先生（Mr. Reeder）震惊地说，"我完全确信格雷先生是清白无辜的。"

===Original===
Jeffrey regarded him with a sneer of contempt.

===Chinese===
杰弗里带着轻蔑的冷笑看着他。

===Original===
"You're a pretty funny 'busy.' I suppose Craig brought you here?"

===Chinese===
"你这个'条子'当得可真逗。我猜是克雷格把你带来的？"

===Original===
"No," murmured Mr. Reeder, "I brought myself here."

===Chinese===
"不，"里德先生低声说，"我自己来的。"

===Original===
"The only thing I can say about you," said Jeffrey Legge, "is that you're smarter than old Golden—and that's not saying much."

===Chinese===
"我唯一能说你的就是，"杰弗里·莱格说，"你比老戈尔登（Golden）强点——不过也强不了多少。"

===Original===
"Not very much," murmured Mr. Reeder.

===Chinese===
"确实强不了多少，"里德先生低声附和。

===Original===
"But you're not smart enough to know that Johnny Gray has been in this business for years."

===Chinese===
"可你还没聪明到发现约翰尼·格雷参与这档子事已经好几年了。"

===Original===
"Even while he was in prison?" suggested Mr. Reeder innocently. "The opportunities are rather restricted, don't you think? But don't let us quarrel, Mr. Jeffrey."

===Chinese===
"连他在坐牢的时候也算？"里德先生天真地问，"机会不是太有限了吗，你不觉得？不过咱们别吵了，杰弗里先生。"

===Original===
The portcullis was raised now, and in a few minutes the girl was in her father's arms.

===Chinese===
吊门已经升起，几分钟后姑娘便扑进了父亲的怀里。

===Original===
"Johnny, you've had a narrow squeak," said Craig, as he shook the man's hand, "and there's some talk about you being in this slush business, but I'll not believe it till I get proof."

===Chinese===
"约翰尼，你这一关算是有惊无险，"克雷格握着他的手说，"有人议论你跟这桩伪钞案子有关，不过在拿到证据之前，我不会信的。"

===Original===
"Who killed old Legge?" asked Johnny.

===Chinese===
"谁杀了老莱格？"约翰尼问。

===Original===
The detective shook his head.

===Chinese===
侦探摇了摇头。

===Original===
"We don't know. But Stevens has disappeared, and Stevens was Fenner's brother. I got it from Mr. Reeder, who seems to have remarkable sources of information."

===Chinese===
"不知道。但史蒂文斯（Stevens）失踪了，而史蒂文斯是芬纳（Fenner）的哥哥。我是听里德先生说的，他似乎有非凡的信息来源。"

===Original===
"Not at all," disclaimed the apologetic Reeder. "I certainly have a remarkable source of information, and to that all credit must go. But I think you will confirm my statement, John, that Stevens is Fenner's brother?"

===Chinese===
"算不上非凡，"谦逊的里德先生连忙否认，"我的确有一个很不一般的信息来源，功劳全在那儿。不过我想你会证实我说的话吧，约翰，说史蒂文斯是芬纳的哥哥？"

===Original===
To Peter's surprise, Johnny nodded.

===Chinese===
令彼得意外的是，约翰尼点了点头。

===Original===
"Yes, I knew they were brothers; and it is unnecessary to say that their name was neither Stevens nor Fenner. It is pretty well established that the old man gave away Fenner—shopped him for the Berkeley Square job—and possibly Stevens got to know of this, and had been waiting his opportunity to settle accounts with Emanuel. Have you caught him?"

===Chinese===
"是的，我知道他们是兄弟。而且不用说，他们既不姓史蒂文斯也不姓芬纳。可以确定的是，老头子出卖了芬纳——为了伯克利广场那桩案子告发了他——史蒂文斯可能知道了这件事，一直在等机会找伊曼纽尔（Emanuel）算账。你们抓到他了吗？"

===Original===
"Not yet," said Craig.

===Chinese===
"还没，"克雷格说。

===Original===
"I hope you won't," said Johnny. "What are you going to do about me, Peter?"

===Chinese===
"但愿你们抓不到，"约翰尼说，"彼得，你打算怎么处置我？"

===Original===
He put his arm round the girl's shoulder, and Peter smiled.

===Chinese===
他搂住姑娘的肩膀，彼得笑了。

===Original===
"I suppose I'll have to let her marry you, Johnny, whether you're a crook or honest. I want you to go straight, and I'll make it worth while—"

===Chinese===
"我想我只能让她嫁给你了，约翰尼，不管你是不是正经人。我希望你走正道，我会让你觉得值——"

===Original===
"That I can promise you." It was Mr. Reeder who spoke. "And may I offer an apology. I'm rather a wolf in sheep's clothing, or a sheep in wolf's clothing. The truth is, my name is Golden."

===Chinese===
"这一点我可以担保。"说话的是里德先生。"请容我道个歉。我算是一只披着羊皮的狼，或者说披着狼皮的羊。真相是，我的真名叫戈尔登（Golden）。"

===Original===
"Golden!" gasped Craig. "But I thought Golden was out of this business?"

===Chinese===
"戈尔登！"克雷格倒吸一口气，"可我以为戈尔登已经退出了这桩买卖？"

===Original===
"He is out of it, and yet he is in it," explained Mr. Reeder carefully. "I am an excellent office man," he confessed, in that mincing manner of his, staring owlishly over his glasses, "but a very indifferent seeker of information, and although, when Mr. John Gray Reeder was appointed over me as chief inspector of my department—"

===Chinese===
"他确实退出了，可又没完全退出，"里德先生仔细地解释道，"我是一个出色的办公室文员，"他以那种扭捏作态的腔调承认道，隔着镜片傻愣愣地瞪着眼，"但搜寻情报的能力实在平平。不过自从约翰·格雷·里德先生被任命为我那个部门的首席督察——"

===Original===
"Here, stop!" said the dazed Craig. "John Gray Reeder? Who is Inspector John Gray Reeder?"

===Chinese===
"等等！"被搞糊涂了的克雷格喊道，"约翰·格雷·里德？约翰·格雷·里德督察是谁？"

===Original===
Mr. Golden's hand went out in the direction of the smiling Johnny.

===Chinese===
戈尔登先生的手指向了微笑的约翰尼。

===Original===
"Johnny! You a 'busy'!" said the bewildered Peter. "But you went to jail sure enough?"

===Chinese===
"约翰尼！你是条子！"彼得大吃一惊，"可你确实坐过牢啊？"

===Original===
"I certainly went to jail," said Johnny. "It was the only place I could get any news about the Big Printer, and I found out all I wanted to know. It was a trying two years, but well worth it, though I nearly lost the only thing in the world that made life worth living," he said. "You've got to forgive me, Peter, because I spied on you—a good spy doesn't play favourites. I've been watching you and every one of your pals, and I watched Marney most of all. And now I'm going to watch her for years and years!"

===Chinese===
"我当然坐了牢，"约翰尼说，"那是唯一能打探到'大印钞师'（the Big Printer）消息的地方，我想知道的全都查到了。两年很难熬，但完全值得——虽然我差点丢了这世上唯一让我觉得活着有意义的东西，"他说，"你得原谅我，彼得，因为我监视了你——好探员不能讲交情。我一直在盯着你和你的每一个同伙，而盯着最久的就是玛妮（Marney）。从今以后我要守着她，守上一辈子！"

===Original===
"You see," said Mr. Golden, who seemed most anxious to exculpate himself from any accusation of cleverness, "I was merely the listener-in, if I may use a newfangled expression, to the information which John broadcasted. I knew all about this marriage, and I was the person who appointed a woman detective to look after her at the Charlton Hotel—but on Johnny's instructions. That is why he was able to prove his alibi, because naturally, that section of the police which knows him, is always ready to prove alibis for other officers of the police who are mistakenly charged with being criminals."

===Chinese===
"你看，"戈尔登先生说，他似乎急于撇清自己有什么了不起，"我不过是——请允许我用一个时髦的说法——约翰广播消息时的接收器。这场婚姻的事我全知道，安排一名女侦探去查尔顿酒店照看她的人也是我——但那全是按约翰尼的指示办的。这就是他能证明自己不在场的原因，因为认识他的那部分警方人员，自然随时准备为被误认为罪犯的其他警察提供不在场证明。"

===Original===
"How did you guess about the prison?"

===Chinese===
"你是怎么猜到他去了监狱的？"

===Original===
"Fenner squeaked," said Mr. Golden with a gesture of deprecation. "'Squeak' is not a word I like, but it is rather expressive. Yes, Fenner squeaked."

===Chinese===
"芬纳招供了，"戈尔登先生做了个无奈的手势，"'招供'这个词我不太喜欢，但它确实很传神。是的，芬纳招供了。"

===Original===
Two happy people drove home together in the car which had brought Marney to Keytown. The country between Oxford and Horsham is the most beautiful in the land. The road passes through great aisles of tall trees, into which a car may be turned and be hidden from the view of those who pass along the road. Johnny slowed the machine at an appropriate spot, and put it toward the thickest part of the wood. And Marney, who sat with folded hands by his side, did not seek any explanation for his eccentricity.

===Chinese===
两个幸福的人坐同一辆车回到了家，就是那辆把玛妮送到基镇（Keytown）的车。牛津和霍舍姆（Horsham）之间的乡间景色是英格兰最美丽的。公路穿过一条条高大乔木构成的长廊，把车拐进去就能避开路上行人的视线。约翰尼在合适的地方放慢了速度，把车开进了林子最密的地方。玛妮双手交叠地坐在他身旁，对他的古怪行径没有追问半个字。

## Endnotes / 尾注

===Original===
1. Flogging. ↩︎

===Chinese===
1. 鞭刑（Flogging）。 ↩︎
